# Per-function extra L2 regions (SA_L2_EXTRA syntax, docs/re/l2-oracle.md): HOOK@ADDR:LEN[:R|W|RW|D]
#
# For memory a function reads or writes that catalog/rwsets.csv cannot name: heap objects behind
# global pointers or behind `this`. ADDR/LEN are expressions evaluated at each case: numbers, registers
# at entry (ecx = this for thiscall), argN (stack arguments), [EXPR] = the dword at EXPR, + and -.
# A declared region is snapshotted, restored between the original and the Rust run, compared after
# both, and counts as declared for the undeclared-write check. tools/e2e/port-l2.sh passes the lines
# whose HOOK is in its hook list.
#
# Lines: the ARCH-1 pilots (CORE-L2) and PORT-CORE (CORE-L2b, below). Pool constructors need none: their
# header is `this` (declared by the hook signature) and the arrays they write are allocated inside the call.
#
# CPool<T> (layouts.csv CPool<int32>, 0x14 bytes): +0 objects, +4 byte map, +8 size, +0xC first free.
# Pools: ped [0xB74490], vehicle [0xB74494], object [0xB7449C].

# thiscall pool methods: the header is `this` (declared by the hook signature); the byte map:
CPedPool::New@[ecx+4]:[ecx+8]:RW
CVehiclePool::New@[ecx+4]:[ecx+8]:RW
CObjectPool::New@[ecx+4]:[ecx+8]:RW
CPedPool::GetAtRef@[ecx+4]:[ecx+8]:R
CObjectPool::GetAt@[ecx+4]:[ecx+8]:R

# cdecl wrappers on CPools::ms_p*Pool: header and byte map
CPed::operator new-anon@[0xB74490]:0x14:RW
CPed::operator new-anon@[[0xB74490]+4]:[[0xB74490]+8]:RW
CPed::operator delete-anon@[0xB74490]:0x14:RW
CPed::operator delete-anon@[[0xB74490]+4]:[[0xB74490]+8]:RW
CPools::GetPedRef@[0xB74490]:0x14:R
CPools::GetPedRef@[[0xB74490]+4]:[[0xB74490]+8]:R
CPools::GetPed@[0xB74490]:0x14:R
CPools::GetPed@[[0xB74490]+4]:[[0xB74490]+8]:R
CPools::GetVehicleRef@[0xB74494]:0x14:R
CPools::GetVehicleRef@[[0xB74494]+4]:[[0xB74494]+8]:R
CPools::GetVehicle@[0xB74494]:0x14:R
CPools::GetVehicle@[[0xB74494]+4]:[[0xB74494]+8]:R
CPools::GetObjectRef@[0xB7449C]:0x14:R
CPools::GetObjectRef@[[0xB7449C]+4]:[[0xB7449C]+8]:R
CPools::GetObject@[0xB7449C]:0x14:R
CPools::GetObject@[[0xB7449C]+4]:[[0xB7449C]+8]:R

# CQuadTreeNode::InitPool (pools.rs, quadtree.rs).
